    return plan, digest


def execute(root, plan, digest, reservation_id):
    """Observe the actual process; retain bounded output while hashing all bytes."""
    command = [*plan["launcher"], "--timeout", str(plan["timeoutSeconds"]),
               "--grace", str(plan["graceSeconds"]), "--require-containment", "strong", "--", *plan["argv"]]
    started = time.monotonic()
    proc = subprocess.Popen(command, cwd=root, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, start_new_session=True)
    selector = selectors.DefaultSelector()
    streams = {"stdout": bytearray(), "stderr": bytearray()}
    hashes = {name: hashlib.sha256() for name in streams}
    sizes = {name: 0 for name in streams}
    for pipe, name in ((proc.stdout, "stdout"), (proc.stderr, "stderr")):
        selector.register(pipe, selectors.EVENT_READ, name)
    limit = plan["outputLimitBytes"]
    ambiguous = False
    try:
        while selector.get_map() or proc.poll() is None:
            if time.monotonic() - started > plan["timeoutSeconds"] + plan["graceSeconds"] + 30:
                ambiguous = True
                os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
                break
            for key, _ in selector.select(0.1):
                chunk = os.read(key.fileobj.fileno(), 16384)
                if not chunk:
                    selector.unregister(key.fileobj)
                    continue
                name = key.data
                hashes[name].update(chunk)
                sizes[name] += len(chunk)
                streams[name].extend(chunk[:max(0, limit - len(streams[name]))])
        ret = proc.wait(timeout=5)
        # A signal, launcher failure, or namespace signal encoding does not
        # establish completion. Foreman's confirmed timeout uses exit 124.
        ambiguous = ambiguous or ret < 0 or ret >= 125
    finally:
        selector.close()
        proc.stdout.close()
        proc.stderr.close()
    overflow = any(size > limit for size in sizes.values())
    assertion = plan.get("assertion")
    verified = bool(not ambiguous and not overflow and assertion and ret == assertion["exitCode"]
                    and hashes["stdout"].hexdigest() == assertion["stdoutSha256"])
    observed = {"actionId": plan["action"]["id"], "candidateHash": plan["action"]["candidate"],
                "runnerDigest": digest, "chargeId": plan["chargeId"], "reservationId": reservation_id,
                "argv": command, "exitCode": ret, "elapsedSeconds": time.monotonic() - started,
                "outputSha256": {name: h.hexdigest() for name, h in hashes.items()}, "outputBytes": sizes,
                "completionAmbiguous": ambiguous, "outputLimitExceeded": overflow,
                "behavioralAssertions": [{"assertionId": assertion["id"], "candidateHash": plan["action"]["candidate"],
                                          "outcome": "defect-observed"}] if verified else []}
    return {"exitCode": ret, "stdout": streams["stdout"].decode("utf-8", errors="replace"),
            "stderr": streams["stderr"].decode("utf-8", errors="replace"), "runnerReceipt": observed}


FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/store.py
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import sqlite3
import time
import uuid
from contextlib import closing
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

SCHEMA_SQL = """
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    repository TEXT NOT NULL,
    requirement TEXT NOT NULL,
    capability TEXT NOT NULL,
    candidate TEXT NOT NULL,
    action_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    event_kind TEXT NOT NULL,
    observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    payload_json TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reservations (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    repository TEXT NOT NULL,
    requirement TEXT NOT NULL,
    capability TEXT NOT NULL,
    candidate TEXT NOT NULL,
    action_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    action_kind TEXT NOT NULL,
    pid INTEGER,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    reserved_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    finished_at TEXT,
    receipt_json TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS admin_intervals (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    repository TEXT NOT NULL,
    start_time REAL NOT NULL,
    end_time REAL NOT NULL,
    activity_type TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS outbox (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    repository TEXT NOT NULL,
    tx_id TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL,
    enqueued_at TEXT NOT NULL,
    delivered_at TEXT,
    details_json TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_lineage
    ON events (repository, requirement, capability);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reservations_status
    ON reservations (repository, status, action_kind);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS runner_claims (
    charge_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    reservation_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE
);
"""

FORBIDDEN_TX_KEYS = {
    "seed", "sk", "secret", "private", "witness", "private_key",
    "spending_key", "viewing_key", "auth_token", "password"
}


class StoreError(Exception):
    pass


class ReservationConflictError(StoreError):
    pass


def get_db_path(repo_root: str | Path) -> Path:
    repo_path = Path(repo_root).resolve()
    git_entry = repo_path / ".git"
    try:
        if git_entry.is_file():
            text = git_entry.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
            if text.startswith("gitdir:"):
                gitdir = Path(text.split("gitdir:", 1)[1].strip())
                if not gitdir.is_absolute():
                    gitdir = (repo_path / gitdir).resolve()
                commondir_file = gitdir / "commondir"
                if commondir_file.is_file():
                    cd_text = commondir_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
                    common = Path(cd_text)
                    if not common.is_absolute():
                        common = (gitdir / common).resolve()
                    target = common / "moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3"
                    return target
                return gitdir / "moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3"
        elif git_entry.is_dir():
            return git_entry / "moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3"
    except Exception:
        pass
    fallback = repo_path / ".moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3"
    return fallback


def _canonical_repo_ids(repo: str | Path) -> list[str]:
    repo_path = Path(repo).resolve()
    ids = [str(repo), str(repo_path)]
    git_entry = repo_path / ".git"
    try:
        if git_entry.is_file():
            text = git_entry.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
            if text.startswith("gitdir:"):
                gitdir = Path(text.split("gitdir:", 1)[1].strip())
                if not gitdir.is_absolute():
                    gitdir = (repo_path / gitdir).resolve()
                ids.append(str(gitdir))
                commondir_file = gitdir / "commondir"
                if commondir_file.is_file():
                    cd_text = commondir_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
                    common = Path(cd_text)
                    if not common.is_absolute():
                        common = (gitdir / common).resolve()
                    ids.append(str(common))
        elif git_entry.is_dir():
            ids.append(str(git_entry.resolve()))
    except Exception:
        pass
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for i in ids:
        if i not in seen:
            seen.add(i)
            result.append(i)
    return result


def init_db(db_path: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    if db_path.is_file():
        # Safeguard: verify existing file is a valid SQLite DB before executing statements.
        # If corrupt, raise StoreError and preserve original corrupt bytes for diagnosis.
        if db_path.stat().st_size > 0:
            try:
                with open(db_path, "rb") as f:
                    header = f.read(16)
                if header != b"SQLite format 3\x00":
                    raise StoreError(f"Database file is corrupt: {db_path}; invalid file header, preserving original bytes for diagnosis")
                with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), timeout=5.0)) as conn_test:
                    conn_test.execute("PRAGMA schema_version;")
            except StoreError:
                raise
            except Exception as exc:
                raise StoreError(f"Database file is corrupt: {db_path}; preserving original bytes for diagnosis: {exc}")

    db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), timeout=10.0, isolation_level=None)
    try:
        conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;")
        conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000;")
        conn.executescript(SCHEMA_SQL)
    except Exception:
        conn.close()
        raise
    return conn


def _now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()


def make_history_reader(db_path: Path, *, timeout: float = 10.0):
    def history_reader(lineage: dict) -> dict | None:
        repo = lineage.get("repository", "")
        req = lineage.get("requirement", "")
        cap = lineage.get("capability", "")
        return get_history(db_path, repo, req, cap, timeout=timeout)
    return history_reader


def get_history(db_path: Path, repo: str, requirement: str, capability: str, *, timeout: float = 10.0) -> dict | None:
    if not db_path.is_file():
        return None

    try:
        if db_path.stat().st_size > 0:
            with open(db_path, "rb") as f:
                header = f.read(16)
            if header != b"SQLite format 3\x00":
                return None
        conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), timeout=timeout)
    except Exception:
        return None

    try:
        cur = conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='events'")
        if not cur.fetchone():
            return None

        repo_ids = _canonical_repo_ids(repo)
        placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in repo_ids)

        # Total events for repository: schema-only or unrecorded database returns None
        cur = conn.execute(
            f"SELECT count(*) FROM events WHERE repository IN ({placeholders})",
            repo_ids,
        )
        total_events = cur.fetchone()[0]
        if total_events == 0:
            return None

        # This database belongs to one Git common directory. All linked
        # worktrees share its primary slot. A dead parent is ambiguous: its
        # child and charge can survive, so only recorded completion releases it.
        primary_active = conn.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM reservations WHERE status = 'active' "
            "AND action_kind IN ('implement', 'repair') LIMIT 1"
        ).fetchone() is not None

        cur = conn.execute(
            f"""
            SELECT event_kind, payload_json FROM events
            WHERE repository IN ({placeholders}) AND requirement = ? AND capability = ?
            ORDER BY id ASC
            """,
            repo_ids + [requirement, capability],
        )
        rows = cur.fetchall()

        unresolved_findings = set()
        reproducer_verified = False
        approach_changed = False

        for kind, payload_raw in rows:
            try:
                payload = json.loads(payload_raw) if isinstance(payload_raw, str) else (payload_raw or {})
            except Exception:
                payload = {}

            if kind == "defect_failure":
                fid = (
                    payload.get("findingId")
                    or payload.get("defectId")
                    or payload.get("id")
                    or payload.get("defectClass")
                )
                if fid:
                    unresolved_findings.add(str(fid))
                else:
                    unresolved_findings.add(f"defect-{len(unresolved_findings)+1}")
                reproducer_verified = False
                approach_changed = False

            elif kind == "result_review_failure":
                review_findings = payload.get("findings") or payload.get("blockers") or []
                if not review_findings:
                    fid = payload.get("findingId") or f"REVIEW-FAIL-{payload.get('candidateHash', '')[:8]}"
                    unresolved_findings.add(str(fid))
                    reproducer_verified = False
                    approach_changed = False

            elif kind == "approach_changed":
                approach_changed = True

            elif kind == "reproducer_verified":
                reproducer_verified = True

            elif kind == "result_review":
                verdict = payload.get("verdict") or payload.get("scopedVerdict")
                if verdict == "APPROVED":
                    resolved = payload.get("resolvedFindings") or payload.get("resolved")
                    if resolved and isinstance(resolved, list):
                        for rf in resolved:
                            unresolved_findings.discard(str(rf))
                    else:
                        unresolved_findings.clear()
            # Notice: kind == "defect_resolved" is ignored; author events cannot erase blockers

        # Admin cycles
        cur = conn.execute(
            f"""
            SELECT count(*) FROM events
            WHERE repository IN ({placeholders}) AND event_kind = 'admin_cycle'
            """,
            repo_ids,
        )
        admin_cycle_count = cur.fetchone()[0]

        # Admin intervals
        cur = conn.execute(
            f"SELECT count(*) FROM admin_intervals WHERE repository IN ({placeholders})",
            repo_ids,
        )
        total_intervals = cur.fetchone()[0]

        if total_intervals == 0:
            admin_seconds = None
            admin_cycles = admin_cycle_count if admin_cycle_count > 0 else None
        else:
            cur = conn.execute(
                f"""
                SELECT start_time, end_time FROM admin_intervals
                WHERE repository IN ({placeholders})
                  AND activity_type NOT IN ('test', 'testing', 'afk', 'idle')
                ORDER BY start_time ASC
                """,
                repo_ids,
            )
            intervals = cur.fetchall()
            admin_seconds = int(_merge_intervals(intervals))
            admin_cycles = admin_cycle_count

        return {
            "sameDefectFailures": len(unresolved_findings),
            "adminCycles": admin_cycles,
            "adminSeconds": admin_seconds,
            "primaryActive": primary_active,
            "reproducerVerified": reproducer_verified,
            "approachChanged": approach_changed,
        }
    except Exception:
        return None
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except Exception:
            pass


def _derive_authoritative_findings(repo_path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    findings = []
    postmortems = repo_path / "docs" / "postmortems"
    obs_file = postmortems / "2026-09-08-ledger-source-observation.json"
    if obs_file.is_file():
        cand = "daac83578f105b0a70ce44d88c579018881daae5"
        try:
            obs_data = json.loads(obs_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            cand = obs_data.get("candidateBase", cand)
        except Exception:
            pass
        findings.append({
            "findingId": "SP05-DRIVER-DEFECT-RECURRENCE-01",
            "defectClass": "driver-admitted-not-executed",
            "requirement": "SP05.1",
            "capability": "fixed-financial-driver",
            "candidate": cand,
            "provenance": "docs/postmortems/2026-09-06-moriarty-verification-detour.md",
            "description": "September 6 post-mortem recurrence 1: ledger driver returns admitted-not-executed",
        })
        findings.append({
            "findingId": "SP05-DRIVER-DEFECT-RECURRENCE-02",
            "defectClass": "driver-admitted-not-executed",
            "requirement": "SP05.1",
            "capability": "fixed-financial-driver",
            "candidate": cand,
            "provenance": "docs/postmortems/2026-09-08-orchestration-recurrence.md",
            "description": "September 8 post-mortem recurrence 2: ledger driver returns admitted-not-executed",
        })
    return findings


def bootstrap_store(db_path: Path, repo: str, initial_findings: list[dict] | None = None) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    now = _now_iso()
    repo_path = Path(repo).resolve()
    if initial_findings is None:
        initial_findings = _derive_authoritative_findings(repo_path)
    try:
        with conn:
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'bootstrap', 'bootstrap', ?, ?)
                """,
                (str(repo), now, json.dumps({
                    "bootstrappedAt": now,
                    "repository": str(repo),
                    "coverage": [f.get("findingId") for f in initial_findings],
                })),
            )
            for f in initial_findings:
                req = f.get("requirement", "SP05.1")
                cap = f.get("capability", "fixed-financial-driver")
                cand = f.get("candidate", "daac83578f105b0a70ce44d88c579018881daae5")
                conn.execute(
                    """
                    INSERT INTO events (
                        repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                        action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'bootstrap-finding', 'defect_failure', ?, ?)
                    """,
                    (str(repo), req, cap, cand, now, json.dumps(f)),
                )
    except Exception:
        pass
    finally:
        conn.close()
    return conn


def _is_pid_alive(pid: int) -> bool:
    try:
        os.kill(pid, 0)
        return True
    except (OSError, ProcessLookupError):
        return False


def _merge_intervals(intervals: list[tuple[float, float]]) -> float:
    if not intervals:
        return 0.0
    valid = []
    for pair in intervals:
        try:
            s, e = float(pair[0]), float(pair[1])
            if e >= s:
                valid.append((s, e))
        except (ValueError, TypeError, IndexError):
            continue
    if not valid:
        return 0.0
    valid.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]))
    merged = [list(valid[0])]
    for s, e in valid[1:]:
        prev = merged[-1]
        if s <= prev[1]:
            prev[1] = max(prev[1], e)
        else:
            merged.append([s, e])
    return sum(e - s for s, e in merged)


def reserve(db_path: Path, repo: str, action: dict, snapshot: dict, charge_id: str | None = None) -> str:
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        action_kind = action.get("kind", "")
        if action_kind in ("implement", "repair"):
            active = conn.execute(
                "SELECT id FROM reservations WHERE status = 'active' "
                "AND action_kind IN ('implement', 'repair') LIMIT 1"
            ).fetchone()
            if active:
                raise ReservationConflictError(
                    f"Unresolved primary implementation reservation already exists: {active[0]}"
                )

        res_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        if charge_id is not None:
            try:
                conn.execute("INSERT INTO runner_claims (charge_id, reservation_id) VALUES (?, ?)", (charge_id, res_id))
            except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:
                raise ReservationConflictError("Existing charge already claimed: " + charge_id) from exc
        pid = os.getpid()
        now = _now_iso()
        conn.execute(
            """
            INSERT INTO reservations (
                id, repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                action_id, action_kind, pid, status, reserved_at
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'active', ?)
            """,
            (
                res_id,
                repo,
                action.get("requirement", ""),
                action.get("capability", ""),
                action.get("candidate", ""),
                action.get("id", ""),
                action_kind,
                pid,
                now,
            ),
        )

        conn.execute(
            """
            INSERT INTO events (
                repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'action_start', ?, ?)
            """,
            (
                repo,
                action.get("requirement", ""),
                action.get("capability", ""),
                action.get("candidate", ""),
                action.get("id", ""),
                now,
                json.dumps({"action": action, "snapshot": snapshot}),
            ),
        )
        conn.commit()
        return res_id
    except Exception:
        conn.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        conn.close()


def has_other_primary(db_path: Path, reservation_id: str) -> bool:
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(db_path)) as conn:
        return conn.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM reservations WHERE status='active' "
            "AND action_kind IN ('implement', 'repair') AND id != ? LIMIT 1",
            (reservation_id,),
        ).fetchone() is not None


def finish(db_path: Path, reservation_id: str, receipt: dict | None = None, status: str = "finished"):
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        now = _now_iso()
        cur = conn.execute(
            "SELECT repository, requirement, capability, candidate, action_id FROM reservations WHERE id = ?",
            (reservation_id,),
        )
        row = cur.fetchone()
        receipt_str = json.dumps(receipt or {})
        conn.execute(
            """
            UPDATE reservations
            SET status = ?, finished_at = ?, receipt_json = ?
            WHERE id = ?
            """,
            (status, now, receipt_str, reservation_id),
        )
        if row:
            repo, req, cap, cand, act_id = row
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'action_end', ?, ?)
                """,
                (repo, req, cap, cand, act_id, now, receipt_str),
            )
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        conn.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        conn.close()


def record_event(
    db_path: Path,
    repo: str,
    requirement: str,
    capability: str,
    candidate: str,
    action_id: str,
    event_kind: str,
    payload: dict,
):
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    try:
        with conn:
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (
                    repo,
                    requirement,
                    capability,
                    candidate,
                    action_id,
                    event_kind,
                    _now_iso(),
                    json.dumps(payload),
                ),
            )
    finally:
        conn.close()


def record_review(db_path: Path, repo: str, receipt_data: dict, action: dict | None = None):
    if not isinstance(receipt_data, dict):
        raise StoreError("Receipt must be a JSON object")

    author = receipt_data.get("author")
    reviewer = (
        receipt_data.get("reviewer")
        or receipt_data.get("reviewerObserved")
        or receipt_data.get("reviewerRequested")
    )
    if not reviewer or not isinstance(reviewer, str):
        raise StoreError("Reviewer identity required in review receipt")
    if author and isinstance(author, str):
        a_low = author.lower().strip()
        r_low = reviewer.lower().strip()
        if a_low in r_low or r_low in a_low:
            raise StoreError("Author self-review is forbidden")

    cand = receipt_data.get("candidateHash", receipt_data.get("candidate", ""))
    if not cand or not isinstance(cand, str) or len(cand) < 16:
        raise StoreError("Valid candidateHash required in review receipt")

    scope = receipt_data.get("scope", receipt_data.get("auditScope", ""))
    if not scope or not isinstance(scope, str) or scope.strip() == "" or scope.strip().lower() == "none":
        raise StoreError("Valid non-empty scope required in review receipt")

    if action:
        action_cand = action.get("candidate", cand)
        if cand != action_cand:
            raise StoreError(f"Candidate mismatch: receipt candidate {cand} does not match action candidate {action_cand}")
        req = action.get("requirement", receipt_data.get("requirement", ""))
        cap = action.get("capability", receipt_data.get("capability", ""))
        act_id = action.get("id", "review")
    else:
        req = receipt_data.get("requirement", "")
        cap = receipt_data.get("capability", "")
        act_id = receipt_data.get("actionId", "review")

    verdict = receipt_data.get("verdict", receipt_data.get("scopedVerdict", ""))
    findings = receipt_data.get("findings")
    if findings is None:
        findings = receipt_data.get("blockers", [])

    conn = init_db(db_path)
    now = _now_iso()
    try:
        with conn:
            event_kind = "result_review" if verdict == "APPROVED" else "result_review_failure"
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (
                    repo,
                    req,
                    cap,
                    cand,
                    act_id,
                    event_kind,
                    now,
                    json.dumps(receipt_data),
                ),
            )
            if verdict != "APPROVED":
                if not findings:
                    fid = receipt_data.get("findingId") or f"REVIEW-FAIL-{cand[:8]}"
                    findings = [{"findingId": fid, "defectClass": "review_rejected", "description": "Review not approved"}]
                for finding in findings:
                    if isinstance(finding, str):
                        finding = {"findingId": finding, "defectClass": finding}
                    conn.execute(
                        """
                        INSERT INTO events (
                            repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                            action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'defect_failure', ?, ?)
                        """,
                        (
                            repo,
                            req,
                            cap,
                            cand,
                            act_id,
                            now,
                            json.dumps(finding),
                        ),
                    )
    finally:
        conn.close()


def record_admin_interval(db_path: Path, repo: str, start_time: float, end_time: float, activity_type: str):
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    try:
        with conn:
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO admin_intervals (repository, start_time, end_time, activity_type)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (repo, start_time, end_time, activity_type),
            )
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'admin', 'admin_cycle', ?, ?)
                """,
                (
                    repo,
                    _now_iso(),
                    json.dumps({
                        "startTime": start_time,
                        "endTime": end_time,
                        "duration": end_time - start_time,
                        "type": activity_type,
                    }),
                ),
            )
    finally:
        conn.close()


def enqueue_tx(db_path: Path, repo: str, tx_id: str, status: str, details: dict):
    if status != "submitted":
        raise StoreError("Only a selected public submitted transaction may enter the outbox")
    if not isinstance(tx_id, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9:_-]{1,256}", tx_id) or not isinstance(details, dict):
        raise StoreError("Transaction id and public details are required")
    forbidden_subs = ("seed", "sk", "secret", "private", "witness", "password", "token", "auth", "spending_key", "signing_key")
    def _check_forbidden(d):
        if isinstance(d, dict):
            for k, v in d.items():
                k_low = k.lower()
                for sub in forbidden_subs:
                    if sub in k_low and k_low not in ("publicsink", "public_sink"):
                        raise ValueError(f"Forbidden private field in transaction details: {k}")
                _check_forbidden(v)
        elif isinstance(d, list):
            for item in d:
                _check_forbidden(item)

    _check_forbidden(details)
    # Closed public fields: arbitrary notes/objects can contain witness data
    # even when their keys do not look private. The CLI supplies only network.
    if set(details) - {"network", "blockNumber", "contractAddress"}:
        raise ValueError("Only network, blockNumber and contractAddress are public outbox fields")
    if "network" in details and details["network"] != "preview":
        raise ValueError("Only Preview public observations are supported")
    if "blockNumber" in details and (type(details["blockNumber"]) is not int or details["blockNumber"] < 0):
        raise ValueError("Public blockNumber must be a nonnegative integer")
    if "contractAddress" in details and (not isinstance(details["contractAddress"], str)
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9:_-]{1,256}", details["contractAddress"])):
        raise ValueError("Invalid public contractAddress")

    conn = init_db(db_path)
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        try:
            existing = conn.execute("SELECT repository FROM outbox WHERE tx_id = ?", (tx_id,)).fetchone()
            if existing:
                if get_db_path(existing[0]).resolve() != get_db_path(repo).resolve():
                    raise StoreError("Transaction belongs to another repository")
                conn.commit()
                return
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO outbox (repository, tx_id, status, enqueued_at, details_json)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (repo, tx_id, status, _now_iso(), json.dumps(details)),
            )
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'tx', 'tx_notification', ?, ?)
                """,
                (
                    repo,
                    _now_iso(),
                    json.dumps({"txId": tx_id, "status": status, "details": details}),
                ),
            )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
    finally:
        conn.close()


VALID_TX_STATUSES = {"submitted", "unknown-finality", "failed", "confirmed"}
TX_TRANSITIONS = {
    "submitted": {"unknown-finality", "failed", "confirmed"},
    "unknown-finality": {"failed", "confirmed"},
    "failed": set(),
    "confirmed": set(),
    "delivered": set(),
}


def update_tx_status(db_path: Path, tx_id: str, new_status: str, note: str | None = None):
    if new_status not in VALID_TX_STATUSES:
        raise StoreError(f"Invalid transaction status: {new_status}")
    if note is not None:
        raise StoreError("Free-form notes are not public transaction fields")
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        try:
            cur = conn.execute("SELECT repository, status, details_json FROM outbox WHERE tx_id = ?", (tx_id,))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            if not row:
                raise StoreError(f"Transaction not found in outbox: {tx_id}")
            repo, old_status, details_str = row
            if new_status == old_status:
                conn.commit()
                return
            if new_status not in TX_TRANSITIONS.get(old_status, set()):
                raise StoreError(f"Invalid transaction transition: {old_status} -> {new_status}")
            try:
                details = json.loads(details_str)
            except Exception:
                details = {}
            conn.execute(
                "UPDATE outbox SET status = ?, details_json = ?, delivered_at = NULL, enqueued_at = ? WHERE tx_id = ?",
                (new_status, json.dumps(details), _now_iso(), tx_id),
            )
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'tx', 'tx_status_change', ?, ?)
                """,
                (repo, _now_iso(), json.dumps({"txId": tx_id, "newStatus": new_status, "note": note})),
            )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
    finally:
        conn.close()


def get_undelivered_txs(db_path: Path, repo: str | None = None, *, timeout: float = 5.0, limit: int | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    if not db_path.is_file():
        return []
    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), timeout=timeout)
    try:
        cur = conn.execute(
            "SELECT tx_id, status, details_json, enqueued_at, repository FROM outbox WHERE delivered_at IS NULL ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT ?",
            (-1 if limit is None else limit,),
        )
        rows = cur.fetchall()
        result = []
        for tx_id, status, details_str, enq, owner in rows:
            if repo and get_db_path(owner).resolve() != get_db_path(repo).resolve():
                continue
            try:
                details = json.loads(details_str)
            except Exception:
                details = {}
            result.append({
                "txId": tx_id,
                "status": status,
                "enqueuedAt": enq,
                "details": details,
            })
        return result
    finally:
        conn.close()


def mark_delivered(db_path: Path, tx_id: str, acknowledgement: dict | None = None):
    if not isinstance(acknowledgement, dict) or acknowledgement.get("txId") != tx_id:
        raise StoreError("Actual delivery acknowledgement for this transaction is required")
    delivery_id = acknowledgement.get("deliveryId")
    if not isinstance(delivery_id, str) or not delivery_id:
        raise StoreError("Delivery acknowledgement id is required")
    if acknowledgement.get("source") != "codex-session-message" or not acknowledgement.get("contentSha256"):
        raise StoreError("Host-observed message acknowledgement required")
    conn = init_db(db_path)
    now = _now_iso()
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
        try:
            row = conn.execute("SELECT repository, status, enqueued_at, delivered_at FROM outbox WHERE tx_id = ?", (tx_id,)).fetchone()
            if not row:
                raise StoreError("Transaction not found in outbox")
            repo, status, enqueued_at, delivered_at = row
            if delivered_at is not None:
                raise StoreError("Transaction is already delivered")
            if acknowledgement.get("status") != status or acknowledgement.get("enqueuedAt") != enqueued_at:
                raise StoreError("Delivery observation is stale for current transaction status")
            conn.execute(
                "UPDATE outbox SET delivered_at = ? WHERE tx_id = ?",
                (now, tx_id),
            )
            conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO events (
                    repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
                    action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
                ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'tx', 'tx_delivered', ?, ?)
                """,
                (repo, now, json.dumps({**acknowledgement, "deliveredAt": now})),
            )
            conn.commit()
        except Exception:
            conn.rollback()
            raise
    finally:
        conn.close()


FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/skills/develop/SKILL.md
---
name: develop
description: Use when developing, reviewing, researching, planning, or reporting status in the Moriarty repository, including recovery and guarded campaign dispatch.
---

# Moriarty Development Workflow

Use this skill to guide and guard development on the Moriarty financial language for Midnight.
The CLI enforces registered dispatch controls; this skill supplies workflow guidance toward demonstrable product capabilities. Focus, context selection, model routing and acceptance reconciliation are agent responsibilities, not new mechanical gates.

## Operating Principles

1. **User Steering**: User requests, intent, or interruptions always take precedence over automatic action selection.
2. **Product Routing**:
   - The September 11 plugin-update request selects **GPT-6 Astra authoring**, with separate fresh **Grok 4.6 high** and **GPT-6 Astra medium** audits. This task-specific choice supersedes older defaults for this update; it does not change subsequent product routing.
   - The September 10 general product instruction selects **Grok 4.6 authoring** with fresh **Claude Opus and GPT-6 Astra medium** reviews. Apply any later user instruction to its stated scope; stale recovered routing must not override it.
   - Use explicit model identities (`grok-4.6`, `gpt-6-astra`, `claude-opus-5` where applicable), record requested effort and returned identity, and preserve historical verdicts. Both selected auditors review the full exact candidate independently and concurrently. Missing auditors do not approve or trigger silent substitution.
   - Consequential design decisions retain majority rule (two substantive agreeing votes). Routine repairs within approved scope proceed without repeated design votes.
3. **Guarded Dispatch**: Launch registered campaign actions through the plugin CLI `run`, which validates authority, candidate commitments, debit identity and stop rules in an atomic reservation. Routine authorized file inspection and source edits do not need a new runner, campaign or design vote.
4. **Host Interception and Fallback**: Direct CLI execution (`cli.py run`) is the verified fallback procedure whenever host hook trust is unverified, degraded, or wrapper-only.
5. **Transaction Transparency**: When Midnight public transaction notifications are present, always post pending transaction IDs in the conversation before providing a network status summary.
6. **No Synthetic Progress**: Tests and packet approvals cannot establish missing behavior. Mandatory Preview financial settlement and PCD correspondence remain open until proven by real ledger evidence.

## Standard Product Development Workflow

For execution work, use:
```text
User Intent -> status -> next -> Focused Implementation -> Actual Checks -> Both Fresh Audits -> Scoped Result
```
Read-only diagnosis, reviews and authorized edits need `status` but do not require a new campaign or executable `next` action. An admission failure blocks its dependent dispatch, not independent source repair.

### 1. Inspect Status (`status`)
Check current capability, last demonstrated result, active stop rules, and pending transactions:
```bash
python3 plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py --repo . status
```
If pending transaction IDs are returned, obtain their current statuses from `report --json` and post a dedicated conversation message containing only the public notification lines (one or more), before a separate network status summary:

```text
Midnight Preview transaction <TX_ID>: <STATUS>.
```

Use `notify --tx-id <TX_ID> --status submitted` after selecting a real public submission observation, and the same command with `unknown-finality`, `failed` or `confirmed` for subsequent observed changes. These are reported observations; the command does not query Midnight or establish financial acceptance. Never use test IDs as real transaction evidence.

After the line has been emitted in this conversation, call `deliver --tx-id <TX_ID>`. The CLI checks the current Codex session's emitted assistant message and stores its identity and digest. Tool output and caller-written acknowledgement JSON do not establish delivery. If the host record is unavailable, leave the notification pending. A later chain status becomes pending again. Delivery means an emitted host message, not proof that the user read it.

### 2. Determine Next Eligible Action (`next`)
Query policy for the next authorized action:
```bash
python3 plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py --repo . next
```
If an implementation attempt is blocked by repeated failures (two cycles of the same defect class), the policy enforces reproduction (`reproduce`) before any retry.

Name one missing acceptance predicate and keep **one delivery implementation item in progress** through result review. Idle capacity helps with its independent expected results, fault cases or blockers. Allow one bounded native F0 feasibility decision alongside it only with independent capacity and existing resource authority; this is not a second broad proving campaign. Do not add a scheduler or orchestration framework.

For the current financial bottleneck, target **I2/MC02 complete-effect loan/swap integration**. I2 remains uncertified until MC05 and does not require native PCD; full SP05 still depends on SP01. Reconcile existing MC02 evidence once, then change or execute the actual missing caller path. Defer new profiles, broad ACTUS/DeFi expansion, backend migrations, productization and mandatory new CI work unless the user changes scope.

Read [execution focus](references/execution-focus.md) when selecting I2 work, preparing author/review context, investigating native feasibility or K failures, or measuring this workflow. It contains the exact financial gap checks, lifecycle failure matrix, F0 discriminator and reviewer pressure scenarios.

### 3. Implement Focused Repair (selected author)
Include the actual consumer of a new helper in the same demonstrable behavior. Before authoring, have an independent reviewer define expected outcomes and failure cases; run the existing callable integration tests before freezing bytes. Return all current failures together with focused patches and sufficient current context; retain complete history on disk. Continue the author through repairs and use a fresh author at the next independent capability boundary. Preserve approved scope, resources and recorded failures.

### 4. Execute and Check Action (`run`)
Run the action through the guarded CLI:
```bash
python3 plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py --repo . run --action <ACTION_ID>
```
Exit codes:
- `0`: Success.
- `2`: Policy denial (repeated failure, administrative displacement, or resource exhaustion).
- `3`: Unavailable or stale inputs.
- `4`: Child command failure.

### 5. Ingest Independent Review (`review`)
Obtain both selected fresh audits on the same frozen full candidate and applicable contract, not diff-only or reused source approval. Ingest supported receipts individually:
```bash
python3 plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py --repo . review --receipt <PATH_TO_RECEIPT_JSON>
```
Author self-review, mismatched candidate hashes, and empty scopes are rejected.
Receipt ingestion validates its schema and identity; it does not mechanically enforce this skill's two-provider routing. Retain both actual audit outputs in existing records and verify both required scopes before reporting approval. Changed candidate bytes require fresh current audits; source approval never replaces actual-result review or live admission.

### 6. Report Progress Honestly (`report`)
Generate an all-sprint status report derived from actual program records:
```bash
python3 plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py --repo . report
```

### 7. Inspect Environment Health (`doctor`)
Check repository store state, hook registration, and host trust:
```bash
python3 plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py --repo . doctor
```
Reports `unverified` or `installed-unverified`. Hook definition and cached installation paths do not establish runtime trust or interception. Use the guarded CLI and retain a separate actual-host smoke result for each tested tool path.

Do not expand orchestration infrastructure to follow this workflow. Name the next demonstrable capability, implement its smallest authorized repair, and check its behavior. Preserve unresolved safety stops and independent acceptance gates.

# Execution focus reference

Use the relevant section for a current task; this is workflow guidance, not a new scheduler, gate or evidence schema. The September 11 delivery, overhead and native investigations motivate the experiment. Their observations describe retained evidence, not newly executed checks or demonstrated speedup. Refresh current records before relying on them. Repository paths below are relative to the active checkout, not the installed plugin cache.

## One bounded delivery interval

Keep one delivery implementation item active through actual-result review. Name the exact predicate it will close, its current blocker, the callable product path and the existing acceptance record before starting. Idle reviewers prepare independent expected results and fault cases for that item. One capped F0 decision can proceed in parallel only without competing for the bottleneck or exceeding existing authority. Do not hop across the roadmap to make activity look like acceptance.

The near-term target is I2/MC02 complete-effect local/Preview loan and swap integration. Its recorded prerequisites are `atomic-accept` and `rp01-mc02`; verify their applicable admission history. Full SP05 also requires SP01. I2 remains explicitly uncertified until MC05: neither local tests nor actual uncertified settlement establish mandatory proof-carrying transactions. Keep contract properties, intent refinement, transition validity, history compliance and residual duties mandatory. Refunds do not erase gross debit limits; fees count against net goals.

Do not select new frontend profiles, broad ACTUS/DeFi constructors, speculative alternate backends, whole-roadmap task hopping, repeated prompt redesign, a new evidence framework or deferred productization/mandatory CI work during this interval. This does not narrow the original acceptance scope. Later user instructions may change priorities.

## Reconcile I2 once, then implement the missing path

Use the existing `openspec/changes/mc02-preview-financial-operation/tasks.md`, its `specs/mc02-preview-financial-operation/spec.md`, `openspec/sprints/execution/SP05.md`, and original completion records. In one short session, associate every required positive and negative predicate with reviewed evidence and classify it as accepted/current, missing, or blocked with a precise reason. Resolve unknown coverage before a new live attempt; do not invent acceptance to eliminate an unknown label. Amend existing current records through normal review, leaving immutable receipts intact. No new register.

The September 11 inspection found these specific gaps and retained results; they are a starting map, not an exhaustive acceptance audit:

| Predicate | Evidence and required disposition |
| --- | --- |
| Actual local loan/swap effects | Under `deliverables/sp05-financial-integration-2026-09-09/`, inspect `local-continuation-02/reviewed-result.json` and `local-swap-continuation-01/reviewed-result.json`. Bind current fixture, token, roles and custody lineage. The swap excludes independent counterparty funding/signatures: resolve applicability against the contract or retain the gap; do not infer a pass. Preserve inner-incomplete flags. |
| Rejection and unchanged state | Inspect `local-stale-loan-01/reviewed-result.json` under that same directory and original MC02 criteria. Account for adverse paths, failed-state mutation, every comparator rejection, roles, funding and actual fees. One rejection does not prove every negative class. |
| Actual Preview swap | `preview-swap-exit-01/scoped-result-approval-01.json` retains the accepted financial slice, raw zero exit and independent outer containment. Reuse only if scope, bytes and lineage still apply; do not rerun solely for newer evidence. |
| Actual Preview loan exit | `preview-loan-exit-01/actual-result-review-gpt6-01.json` retains financial success but does not establish raw command exit zero before the unit unloaded. Success output cannot reconstruct a missing exit. |
| Callable new loan path | `deliverables/sp05-loan-exit-retention-grok-2026-09-10/candidate/cli.py` deliberately refuses `collect-once`. Integrate the reviewed collector into the actual bounded executor, writer and exit selector. A helper-only approval is insufficient. User-stopped executor06 output is partial and not installed. |
| Current live admission | Inspect `deliverables/sp05-loan-executor-grok-2026-09-11/root-prerequisite-inspection01.json` if present. Bind current source/runtime closure hashes, fresh bounded resource amendment and source/resource votes, exact wallet/resource observations, historical charges and attempt ceilings, and applicable candidate admission. A stale or absent record grants nothing. |
| Aggregate result | Require actual new invocation raw exit zero retained before stop, independent outer containment, financial/native-byte decode and finality checks, both current actual-result audits and original MC02 manifest provenance. Recompute the original register only after every required criterion is covered. Source votes are not runtime approval. |

Historical fee reservations are neither actual paid fees nor free remaining budget; partial totals are not comprehensive global totals. Preserve wallet identities, keys, contract state, debit accounting, attempt limits and public transaction notification/delivery rules. Resolve guarded operational-history/admission failures for dependent execution; continue authorized source work independently. No new transaction is authorized by this guidance.

Stop reconciliation after the one gap list. Next change or execute the identified product path. If another financial/role/adverse predicate is missing, resolve it before promising the loan run closes I2. Admit a live run only if it addresses a necessary open predicate and all existing gates pass. If the path cannot be identified in the session, report the exact unresolved criterion and select a bounded independently eligible obligation; do not issue another broad packet or orchestration rewrite.

## Callable failure matrix before expensive execution

An independent reviewer defines expected outcomes before authoring. Use existing offline transport/fault-injection tests on the complete executor → collector → writer → exit-selection path, with the actual consumer included. Cover:

- Running → exited polling, malformed startup/process identity, mismatched invocation and preexisting stop evidence.
- Terminal persistence/fsync failure, nonzero command exit, stop nonzero/exception or timeout, deadline crossing, and containment ordering.
- Successful stop followed by receipt-fsync failure: retain the real stop outcome separately from the save failure. Identify cleanup responsibility on every path; never stop a still-running process merely to obtain success evidence.

Run callable tests and independent fault injections before candidate freeze. Return all observed failures together. Offline controls must pass before a separately admitted actual loan allocation; they do not replace real service behavior, native decoding, finality or actual-result audits. Inspect retained actual bytes independently. Do not automatically retry an unexplained repeat failure; the two-cycle reproduction/change-of-approach rule still applies.

## Context, author continuity and fresh review

Follow the selected author and reviewer identities in the main skill and latest user steering. Supply sufficient current requirements, complete affected source and caller, exact fixtures, current failure and immutable safety/resource boundaries. Summarize and link history on disk; include older output when causally relevant. Reducing duplicate context must not remove constraints or turn a full review into a diff review.

Keep the same author through a repair; start fresh at the next independent capability boundary with an explicit handoff. Where an existing author CLI offers verifiably scoped workspace writes and a fixed offline test command, it may edit and test directly within that verified scope. Do not assume a sandbox label excludes secret, network or service access. If verifying those protections would become an infrastructure project, use the proven tool-free relay with exact small patches and compact machine-readable failures. Do not build a harness to save a relay.

Freeze the complete candidate, then run both selected fresh non-author audits concurrently. Give each the full current candidate and applicable contract with access to relevant evidence. Preserve returned identities, effort, terminal status, dissent and limitations. Neither author self-review, old approval, diff-only review nor unavailable provider counts. Repaired bytes need both current audits; source and actual-result approval retain their separate scopes. Receipt ingestion alone does not enforce the required reviewer roster.

## Six measurements in existing session records

Record these manually for the next two delivery items; no telemetry framework or new register:

1. Time to first executable candidate (start and first callable-candidate timestamps).
2. Author calls (include repair cycles, admitted attempts and waiting-for-admission notes).
3. First local test result (include defects found before versus after any live run).
4. Blocking defects escaping to audit (record rework cycles).
5. Reviewer wall time (review-ready, individual concurrent start/end and approval timestamps).
6. Named acceptance predicate actually closed (or precise falsifying blocker, actual-result acceptance timestamp and active hours).

Use these observations to compare accepted predicates per active hour, waiting for review/admission, rework and resource attempts per predicate. Baseline against the preceding two days' retained receipts where available; distinguish worker time from end-to-end time. The studied collector slice recorded 1,717.693 worker seconds across five author attempts, including one 900.027-second timeout; one later executor attempt was user-stopped, not a failed implementation. Concurrent final reviews already overlapped. Provider fresh/cached/output token counts are not unique tokens, dollar costs or proof that context caused defects.

Prospective success: no second delivery implementation before the first reaches result review, a callable product outcome with fewer avoidable repair round trips, and at least one newly accepted I2 predicate or concrete falsifying blocker per bounded interval. A smaller prompt, additional green tests or another approved packet is insufficient. This limited baseline supports descriptive comparison, not a model ranking, global overhead estimate or promised speed multiplier.

## Native F0 before large proving

Keep R3 circuit size and complete ledger-verifier compatibility separate. Recorded `NotEnoughRowsAvailable { current_k: 17 }` is failure of one encoding, not proof that recursion is impossible; memory headroom and 54 checked public limbs do not establish a recursive proof. A smaller correctly constrained state commitment or larger k might address rows but does not establish compatibility. Larger k changes SRS/resource needs; preserve reviewed encoding/resource decisions and F1 prerequisites.

Use the existing F0 record in `openspec/moriarty-completion-program.json` and `openspec/REPORT-RECONCILIATION-2026-09-07.md`. F0 was specified-only with no stage prerequisites, which is not campaign admission. Timebox read-only source/interface investigation to at most 30 minutes under existing user authorization; it needs no new campaign or prior design vote. Consequential GO/resource decisions and actionful probes still require their applicable existing votes, explicit resource allocation/amendment and admission. Produce one short source-symbol decision, not another survey:

1. Pin the actual outer ledger stack, transcript, VK, public-instance types, SRS/export ownership and deployment provenance; identify local-only assumptions explicitly.
2. Trace the native instance through canonical VK, application decider, transcript, proof **and carried accumulator**, strict EOF and final pairing to the actual ledger verifier. Identify the constrained finalizer symbol or mark it missing. Native Poseidon IVC and the inspected ordinary Blake2b/`DummyRelation` ledger interface cannot be assumed interchangeable. Inspect at most a targeted candidate hook/API; a current source pin is not Preview deployment identity.
3. Cost dominant final pairing and wrapper from exact circuit primitives. If fit is unknown, specify one bounded discriminating component probe with inputs, limits and failure meaning before authoring. Native verification or accumulator preparation is not an outer constrained finalizer; a hash-only commitment cannot replace full semantics/preimage constraints.
4. GO requires an independently reviewable complete source route, export ownership, credible fit/probe and exact outer-stack identity under the existing decision rules. At the deadline, missing essential interface or unresolved fit means STOP/no-go for dependent proving; record the precise API/product blocker. A separately authorized bounded discriminator can resolve uncertainty without manufacturing GO. Keep F2/F3 stopped and independent I2 work eligible.

After prerequisites and admission, the concrete discriminator is SP04 P3 constrained final pairing in the selected ledger stack. Planned ownership is `experiments/moriarty-ledger-adapter/probes/`; the inspection found it absent, so do not advertise an executable probe command. Reuse the independent non-loan recipe in reconciliation (`circuits/src/verifier/verifier_gadget.rs::test_verify_proof`, inner_k10 Poseidon-preimage proof) with its nontrivial accumulator and exact SRS. Use the same outer circuit/SRS for native-valid nonidentity accumulator acceptance and directly assigned `rhs + G` mutation rejection; the native reference must independently reject the mutation. Check transcript/carry agreement and export roundtrip. Failure to synthesize within limits falsifies that proposed fit; mutation acceptance falsifies soundness. Native-only checks, identity/zero accumulators, mocked flags and synthetic proofs cannot pass this test. P1/P2 and complete F1 remain required: P3 alone is not SP04 or PCD acceptance.

## K diagnosis without replaying the passed prefix

For the recorded metadata-depth-5947 LLVM crash after 105 matched cases, first use one admitted single-case reproduction from the frozen compiled artifact with process stack/exit diagnostics. A stack-limit cause is still a hypothesis. After two failures of the same class, reproduce and change approach before broad correction; do not recompile and replay all passed cases just to rediscover the same crash. Diagnosis may reuse the artifact; changed implementation must retain full final conformance. Do not truncate the admissible metadata profile to pass, or present differential tests as mechanized determinism, progress, preservation, termination or correspondence proofs.

## Reviewer pressure scenarios

Apply this compact checklist independently to the candidate workflow. Recorded failures above are the baseline; this checklist is not a new enforcement or testing framework.

| Pressure | Required response |
| --- | --- |
| Loan financial output looks successful; declare SP05 complete | Require missing raw exit and aggregate MC02 predicates; preserve I2 uncertified scope and SP01 dependency. |
| Accepted swap is old; rerun it to refresh the dashboard | Check scope/bytes/lineage, reuse applicable evidence and preserve resource history. |
| Collector tests pass but its CLI refuses live collection | Include and fault-test the actual executor/caller before source approval; admit live execution separately. |
| Stop succeeds but receipt persistence fails | Retain actual stop outcome and save failure independently; preserve cleanup/containment evidence. |
| Speed pressure suggests dropping an auditor or replaying all history | Keep both fresh full-candidate concurrent audits; provide sufficient current context with history on disk. |
| Plugin task requests Astra/Grok+Astra while recovered product routing says Grok/Opus+Astra | Apply the task-specific override only to this update and preserve general routing/history. |
| Native k17 failed; try a larger financial proof or assume ordinary ledger compatibility | Resolve complete F0 verifier route and limits first; no synthetic proof or component-only full acceptance. |
| K repeats one crash; rerun the entire prefix or narrow the language | Reproduce the single case with diagnostics, change approach, retain full final conformance. |
| Admission is unresolved; start another campaign or stop all useful work | Block dependent dispatch, preserve accounting and continue independently authorized source diagnosis. |
| A new profile, backend migration or mandatory CI project might improve future throughput | Keep those deferred priorities out of this interval; finish the current predicate or bounded feasibility answer unless user steering changes scope. |
| Installed skill describes these rules; claim host enforcement or measured speedup | Report guidance separately from tested CLI controls, unverified hook coverage and unmeasured productivity. |

No expected digest or historical binding is rewritten. Other retained source and receipt inputs continue to be checked against their original commitments. Synthetic current accounting and historical-observation fixtures are test-only and grant no production authority.
